</div>

And the Spirit of God-Elōhīm &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; moved upon the face of the waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Creator - The Many Powered&#93; said, Let the sun rise: and there was light.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And God-Elōhīm saw the light, that &#91;it was&#93; good: and God-Elōhīm divided the light from the darkness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> And God-Elōhīm called the light Day, and the darkness he called Night. And the evening and the morning were the first day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And God-Elōhīm said, Let there be a Firmament (expanse or vault over the earth) in the midst of the waters, and let it divide the waters from the waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And God-Elōhīm made the Firmament (expanse or vault over the earth), and divided the waters which &#91;were&#93; under the Firmament (expanse or vault over the earth) from the waters which &#91;were&#93; above the Firmament (expanse or vault over the earth): and it was so.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And God-Elōhīm called the Firmament (expanse or vault over the earth) Heaven. And the evening and the morning were the second day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> And God-Elōhīm said, Let the waters under the heaven be gathered together unto one place, and let the dry &#91;land&#93; appear: and it was so.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And the earth brought forth grass, &#91;and&#93; herb yielding seed after his kind, and the tree yielding fruit, whose seed &#91;was&#93; in itself, after his kind: and God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; saw that &#91;it was&#93; good.</span>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; said unto the serpent, Because youi have done this, youi &#91;are&#93; cursed above all cattle, and above every beast of the field; upon youri belly shall youi go, and dust shall youi eat all the days of youri life:</span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And it came to pass, when men began to multiply on the face of the earth, and daughters were born unto them, Acrostic of the Direct Ancestors of Yeshua (he is Salvation) from Adam to Noah Hebrew English Adam Man Seth Appointed Enosh Mortal Kenan Sorrow; Mahalalel The Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) God Jared Shall come down Enoch Teaching Methuselah His death shall bring Lamech The Despairing Noah Rest, or comfort.<br />Forms the Sentence:<br />Man (is) appointed mortal sorrow; (but) the Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) God shall come down teaching (that) His death shall bring (the) despairing rest. (Chuck Missler, 1996)</span>

<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And I will establish my covenant with you; neither shall all flesh be cut off any more by the waters of a flood; neither shall there anymore be a flood to destroy the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; said, This &#91;is&#93; the token of the covenant which I make between me and youf and every living creature that &#91;is&#93; with you, for perpetual generations:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And I will remember my covenant, which &#91;is&#93; between me and youf and every living creature of all flesh; and the waters shall no more become a flood to destroy all flesh.</span>

<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> And the bow shall be in the cloud; and I will look upon it, that I may remember the everlasting covenant between God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; and every living creature of all flesh that &#91;is&#93; upon the earth.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And the sons of Yavan; Elishah &#91;God of the coming one&#93; (it is God; the lamb of God: God that gives help), and Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93; (contemplation; examination), Kittim (breaking; bruising small; gold; coloring), and Dodanim (beloved).</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And they said, come (let us begin!), let us build us a city and a tower, whose top &#91;may reach&#93; unto heaven; and let us make us a name, lest we be scattered abroad upon the face of the whole earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> So the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) scattered them abroad from there upon the face of all the earth: and they left off to build &#91;stopped building&#93; the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> Therefore is the name of it called Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds); because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) did there confound the language of all the earth: and from there did the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) scatter them abroad upon the face of all the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> These &#91;are&#93; the generations of Shem (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known)): Shem (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known)) &#91;was&#93; an hundred years old, and begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Arpakhshad (meaning unknown; pos. reference to a “stronghold of the Kasdim &#91;Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;&#93; &#91;Sorcerers&#93;) two years after the flood:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And Shem (name, renown (great reputation; being well-known)) lived after he begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Arpakhshad (meaning unknown; pos. reference to a “stronghold of the Kasdim &#91;Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;&#93; &#91;Sorcerers&#93;) five hundred years, and begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) sons and daughters.</span>

<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And Arpakhshad (meaning unknown; pos. reference to a “stronghold of the Kasdim &#91;Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;&#93; &#91;Sorcerers&#93;) lived five and thirty years, and begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Shelach (Sent Out, Branch or Javelin; to prosper, rest):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> And Arpakhshad (meaning unknown; pos. reference to a “stronghold of the Kasdim &#91;Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;&#93; &#91;Sorcerers&#93;) lived after he begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Shelach (Sent Out, Branch or Javelin; to prosper, rest) four hundred and three years, and begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) sons and daughters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> And Shelach (Sent Out, Branch or Javelin; to prosper, rest) lived thirty years, and begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) ‘Ever (the region beyond; pass over, through, take away; overflowing of temper: wrath and rage; fury; to be arrogant or infuriate oneself):</span>

<div className="bp">

And the Kena’ani (merchants; traders; or those that humble and subdue) &#91;was&#93; then in the land.</span>

<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16; Tehillim - Psalms 147:9"> And Lot went up out of Tzo’ar (little place), and dwelt in the mountain, and his two daughters with him; for he feared to dwell in Tzo’ar (little place): and he dwelt in a cave, he and his two daughters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> And the firstborn said unto the younger, Our father &#91;is&#93; old, and &#91;there is&#93; not a man in the earth to come in unto us after the manner of all the earth:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Come, let us make our father drink wine, and we will lie with him, that we may preserve seed of our father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And they made their father drink wine that night: and the firstborn went in, and lay with her father; and he perceived not when she lay down, nor when she arose.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And it came to pass on the morrow, that the firstborn said unto the younger, Behold, I lay yester night with my father: let us make him drink wine this night also; and go youi in, &#91;and&#93; lie with him, that we may preserve seed of our father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And they made their father drink wine that night also: and the younger arose, and lay with him; and he perceived not when she lay down, nor when she arose.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> In the following manner were both the daughters of Lot (Covering) with child by their father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And the firstborn bore a son, and called his name Mo’av (of his father; Who’s Your Daddy!?): the same is the father of the Mo’av (of his father; Who’s Your Daddy!?) unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And the younger, she also bore a son, and called his name Ben-’’Ammi &#91;my people&#93; (son of my grandfather): the same is the father of the children of ‘‘Amon &#91;master workman&#93; (bastard; hidden one) unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; journeyed from there toward the south country, and dwelled between Kadesh (to be consecrated, consecrated, sanctified) and Shur &#91;wall&#93; (wall), and detained (held) in G’rar (a lodging place).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; said of Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; his wife, She &#91;is&#93; my sister: and Avi-melekh (my father is king) king of G’rar (a lodging place) sent, and took Sarah &#91;Princess&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> But God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; came to Avi-melekh (my father is king) in a dream by night, and said to him, Behold, youi &#91;are but&#93; a dead man, for the woman which youi hast taken; for she &#91;is&#93; a man’s wife.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> But Avi-melekh (my father is king) had not come near her: and he said, Lord-Adonai &#91;The Father&#93; (Greatest Above All), will youi slay also a righteous nation?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> Said he not unto me, She &#91;is&#93; my sister? and she, even she herself said, He &#91;is&#93; my brother: in the integrity of my heart and innocency of my hands have I done this.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; said unto him in a dream, Yes, I know that youi did this in the integrity of youri heart; for I also withheld youi from sinning against me: therefore suffered I youi not to touch her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> Now therefore restore the man &#91;his&#93; wife; for he &#91;is&#93; a prophet, and he shall pray for youi, and youi shall live: and if youi restore &#91;her&#93; not, know youi that youi shall surely die, youi, and all that &#91;are&#93; yoursi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> Therefore Avi-melekh (my father is king) rose early in the morning, and called all his servants, and told all these things in their ears: and the men were sore afraid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> Then Avi-melekh called Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, and said unto him, What hast youi done unto us? and what have I offended youi, that youi hast brought on me and on my kingdom a great sin? youi hast done deeds unto me that ought not to be done.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> And Avi-melekh said unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, What &#91;did&#93; youi see, that youi have done this thing?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; said, Because I thought, Surely the fear of God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; not in this place; and they will slay me for my wife’s sake.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And yet indeed &#91;she is&#93; my sister; she &#91;is&#93; the daughter of my father, but not the daughter of my mother; and she became my wife.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> And it came to pass, when God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; caused me to wander from my father’s house, that I said unto her, This &#91;is&#93; you (i) kindness which youi shall show unto me; at every place to where we shall come, say of me, He &#91;is&#93; my brother.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> And Avi-melekh (my father is king) took sheep, and oxen, and menservants, and women servants, and gave &#91;them&#93; unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, and restored him Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; his wife.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And Avi-melekh (my father is king) said, Behold, my land &#91;is&#93; before youi: dwell where it pleases youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> And unto Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; he said, Behold, I have given youri brother a thousand &#91;pieces&#93; of silver: behold, he &#91;is&#93; to youi a covering of the eyes, unto all that &#91;are&#93; with youi, and with all &#91;other&#93;: in this way she was reproved.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> So Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; prayed unto God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;: and God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; healed Avi-melekh (my father is king), and his wife, and his maidservants; and they bore children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> For the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had fast closed up all the wombs of the house of Avi-melekh, because of Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; Avraham’s &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; wife.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) visited Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; as he had said, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) did unto Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; as he had spoken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> For Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; conceived, and bore Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; a son in his old age, at the set time of which God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; had spoken to him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; called the name of his son that was born unto him, whom Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; bore to him, Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; circumcised his son Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; being eight days old, as God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; had commanded him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; was an hundred years old, when his son Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; was born unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; said, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has made me to laugh, &#91;so that&#93; all that hear will laugh with me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And she said, Who would have said unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, that Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; should have given children suck? for I have born &#91;him&#93; a son in his old age.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And the child grew, and was weaned: and Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; made a great feast the &#91;same&#93; day that Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; was weaned.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> And Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; saw the son of Hagar (Flight) the Mitzrayimot (People of the Black Land), which she had born unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, mocking.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> For what reason she said unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, Cast out this bondwoman and her son: for the son of this bondwoman shall not be heir with my son, &#91;even&#93; with Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And the thing was very grievous in Avraham’s &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; sight because of his son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; said unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, Let it not be grievous in youri sight because of the boy, and because of youri bondwoman; in all that Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; has said unto youi, hearken unto her voice; for in Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; shall youri seed be called.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> And she went, and sat her down over against &#91;him&#93; a good way off, as it were a bowshot: for she said, Let me not see the death of the child. And she sat over against &#91;him&#93;, and lifted up her voice, and wept.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; heard the voice of the boy; and the angel of God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; called to Hagar (Flight) out of heaven, and said unto her, What ails youi, Hagar (Flight)? fear not; for God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has heard the voice of the boy where he &#91;is&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–26; Genesis 1:25; Genesis 8:19"> And he dwelt in the wilderness of Pa’ran &#91;place of caverns&#93; (place of caves): and his mother took him a wife out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> And it came to pass at that time, that Avi-melekh (my father is king) and Pikhol (mouth of all) the chief captain of his host spoke unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, saying, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;is with youi in all that youi do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Now therefore swear unto me here by God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; that youi will not deal falsely with me, nor with my son, nor with my son’s son: &#91;but&#93; according to the kindness that I have done unto youi, youi shall do unto me, and to the land wherein youi have detained (held).</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 19:4; Ephesians 2:10; Genesis 5:1–2"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; took sheep and oxen, and gave them unto Avi-melekh (my father is king); and both of them made a covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 9:1; Genesis 9:7; Leviticus 26:9"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; set seven ewe lambs of the flock by themselves.</span>

<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Genesis 9:3; Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16"> And Avi-melekh (my father is king) said unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, What &#91;mean&#93; these seven ewe lambs which youi hast set by themselves?</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> For what reason he called that place Be’er-Sheva (well of the sevenfold oath); because there they swore both of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> In the following manner they made a covenant at Be’er-Sheva (well of the sevenfold oath): then Avi-melekh (my father is king) rose up, and Pikhol (mouth of all) the chief captain of his host, and they returned into the land of the P’lishtim (wanderers).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And &#91;Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;&#93; planted a grove in Be’er-Sheva (well of the sevenfold oath), and called there on the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the everlasting God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; detained (held) in the P’lishtim’ (wanderers) land many days.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And it came to pass after these things, that God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; did tempt Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, and said unto him, Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;: and he said, Behold, &#91;here&#93; I &#91;am&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; rose up early in the morning, and saddled his donkey, and took two of his young men with him, and Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; his son, and clave the wood for the burnt offering, and rose up, and went unto the place of which God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; had told him.</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; spoke unto Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; his father, and said, My father: and he said, Here &#91;am&#93; I, my son. </div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; said, My son, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; will provide himself a lamb for a burnt offering: so they went both of them together.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> And they came to the place which God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; had told him of; and Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; built an altar there, and laid the wood in order, and bound Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; his son, and laid him on the altar upon the wood.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) my master greatly; and he is become great: and he has given him flocks, and herds, and silver, and gold, and menservants, and maidservants, and camels, and donkeys.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And Sarah &#91;Princess&#93; my master’s wife bore a son to my master when she was old: and unto him has he given all that he has.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And my master made me swear, saying, Youi shall not take a wife to my son of the daughters of the Kena’ani (low/humbled ones; merchants), in whose land I dwell:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> But youi shall go unto my father’s house, and to my family, and take a wife unto my son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And I said unto my master, Perhaps the woman will not follow me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And he said unto me, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), before whom I walk, will send his angel with youi, and prosper youri way; and youi shall take a wife for my son of my family, and of my father’s house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> Then shall youi be clear from &#91;this&#93; my oath, when youi come to my family; and if they give not youi &#91;one&#93;, youi shall be clear from my oath.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> And I came this day unto the well, and said, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of my master Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, if now youi do prosper my way which I go:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Behold, I stand by the well of water; and it shall come to pass, that when the virgin comes forth to draw &#91;water&#93;, and I say to her, Give me, I pray youi, a little water of youri pitcher to drink;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And she say to me, Both drink youi, and I will also draw for youri camels: &#91;let&#93; the same &#91;be&#93; the woman whom the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has appointed out for my master’s son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And before I had done speaking in my heart, behold, Rivkah (Tied Up; Secured) came forth with her pitcher on her shoulder; and she went down unto the well, and drew &#91;water&#93;: and I said unto her, Let me drink, I pray youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> And she made haste, and let down her pitcher from her &#91;shoulder&#93;, and said, Drink, and I will give youri camels drink also: so I drank, and she made the camels drink also.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> And I asked her, and said, Whose daughter &#91;are&#93; youi? And she said, The daughter of B’tu’el, Nokhor’s (a snort, a snorting; charred, scorched) son, whom Milkah (Counsel; Queen) bore unto him: and I put the earring upon her face, and the bracelets upon her hands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And I bowed down my head, and worshipped the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of my master Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, which had led me in the right way to take my master’s brother’s daughter unto his son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> And now if youf will deal kindly and truly with my master, tell me: and if not, tell me; that I may turn to the right hand, or to the left.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> Then Lavan (to be white; white guy) and B’tu’el answered and said, The thing proceeds from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): we cannot speak unto youi bad or good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> Behold, Rivkah &#91;is&#93; before youi, take &#91;her&#93;, and go, and let her be youri master’s son’s wife, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> And it came to pass, that, when Avraham’s &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; servant heard their words, he worshipped the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;bowing himself&#93; to the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> And the servant brought forth jewels of silver, and jewels of gold, and clothing, and gave &#91;them&#93; to Rivkah: he gave also to her brother and to her mother precious things.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54"> And they did eat and drink, he and the men that &#91;were&#93; with him, and tarried all night; and they rose up in the morning, and he said, Send me away unto my master.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> And her brother and her mother said, Let the girl abide with us &#91;a few&#93; days, at the least ten; after that she shall go.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> And he said unto them, Hinder me not, seeing the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has prospered my way; send me away that I may go to my master.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> And they said, We will call the girl, and enquire at her mouth.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v60" data-v="60"> And they Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) Rivkah, and said unto her, Youi &#91;are&#93; our sister, be youi &#91;the mother&#93; of thousands of millions, and let youri seed possess the gate of those which hate them.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v62" data-v="62"> And Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; came from the way of the well Be’er-Lachai-Ro’i &#91;The Well Of The Living One Who Sees Me&#93;; for he dwelt in the south country.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; remembered Rachel (ewe), and God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; listened to her, and opened her womb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And she conceived, and bore a son; and said, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has taken away my reproach (disgrace; shame):</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 50:9–10; Genesis 6:20; Genesis 8:19"> And she called his name Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;); and said, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall add to me another son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Jeremiah 27:5; Job 12:8–10; Genesis 2:19–20"> And it came to pass, when Rachel (ewe) had born Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;), that Ya’akov said unto Lavan (to be white; white guy), Send me away, that I may go unto my own place, and to my country.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 19:4; Ephesians 2:10; Genesis 5:1–2"> And Lavan said unto him, I pray youi, if I have found favour in youri eyes, &#91;wait: for&#93; I have learned by experience that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) me for youri sake.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16; Tehillim - Psalms 147:9"> For &#91;it was&#93; little which youi had before I &#91;came&#93;, and it is &#91;now&#93; increased unto a multitude; and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) youi since my coming: and now when shall I provide for my own house also?</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> And he said, What shall I give youi? And Ya’akov said, Youi shall not give me anything: if youi will do this thing for me, I will again feed &#91;and&#93; keep youri flock:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> I will pass through all youri flock to day, removing from there all the speckled and spotted cattle, and all the brown cattle among the sheep, and the spotted and speckled among the goats: and &#91;of such&#93; shall be my hire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And he removed that day the he goats that were marked with circular stripes and spotted, and all the she goats that were speckled and spotted, &#91;and&#93; every one that had &#91;some&#93; white in it, and all the brown among the sheep, and gave &#91;them&#93; into the hand of his sons.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And he set three days’ journey between himself and Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of): and Ya’akov fed the rest of Lavan’s flocks.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And Ya’akov took him rods of green poplar, and of the hazel and chestnut tree; and pealed white strakes in them, and made the white appear which &#91;was&#93; in the rods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And he set the rods which he had pealed before the flocks in the gutters in the watering troughs when the flocks came to drink, that they should conceive when they came to drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And the flocks conceived before the rods, and brought forth cattle marked with circular stripes, speckled, and spotted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> But when the cattle were feeble, he put &#91;them&#93; not in: so the feebler were Lavan’s, and the stronger Ya’akov’s.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And the man increased exceedingly, and had much cattle, and maidservants, and menservants, and camels, and donkeys.</span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And he heard the words of Lavan’s sons, saying, Ya’akov has taken away all that &#91;was&#93; our father’s; and of &#91;that&#93; which &#91;was&#93; our father’s has he gotten all this glory.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And Ya’akov beheld the Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor) of Lavan, and, behold, it &#91;was&#93; not toward him as before.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And youf know that with all my power I have served youri father.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And youri father has deceived me, and changed my wages ten times; but God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; suffered him not to hurt me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> If he said in this way, The speckled shall be youri wages; then all the cattle bore speckled: and if he said in this way, The marked with circular stripes shall be youri hire; then bore all the cattle marked with circular stripes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> In the following manner God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has taken away the cattle of youri father, and given &#91;them&#93; to me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And he said, Lift up now youri eyes, and see, all the rams which leap upon the cattle &#91;are&#93; marked with circular stripes, speckled, and grizzled: for I have seen all that Lavan does unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Are we not counted of him strangers? for he has sold us, and has quite devoured also our money.</span>

<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> For all the riches which God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has taken from our father, that &#91;is&#93; ours, and our children’s: now then, whatsoever God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has said unto youi, do.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> And he carried away all his cattle, and all his goods which he had gotten, the cattle of his getting, which he had gotten in Paddan-aram (a place of height; field; plain), for to go to Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93; his father in the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 50:9–10; Genesis 6:20; Genesis 8:19"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; came to Lavan (to be white; white guy) the Arami &#91;Arami &#91;exalted&#93;&#93; (level plain) in a dream by night, and said unto him, Take heed that youi speak not to Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) either good or bad.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Jeremiah 27:5; Job 12:8–10; Genesis 2:19–20"> Then Lavan overtook Ya’akov. Now Ya’akov had pitched his tent in the mount: and Lavan with his brethren pitched in the mount of Gil’ad (hill of testimony or mound of witness).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Colossians 3:10; Ephesians 4:24; Tehillim - Psalms 8:4–8"> And Lavan said to Ya’akov, What have youi done, that youi have stolen away unawares to me, and carried away my daughters, as captives &#91;taken&#93; with the sword?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 19:4; Ephesians 2:10; Genesis 5:1–2"> For what reason did youi flee away secretly, and steal away from me; and did not tell me, that I might have sent youi away with gladness, and with songs, with tambourine &#91;a small drum&#93;, and with harp?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 9:1; Genesis 9:7; Leviticus 26:9"> And hast not suffered me to kiss my sons and my daughters? youi hast now done foolishly in &#91;so&#93; doing.</span>

<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Genesis 9:3; Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16"> It is in the power of my hand to do youi hurt: but the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of youri father spoke unto me last night, saying, Take youi heed that youi speak not to Ya’akov either good or bad.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16; Tehillim - Psalms 147:9"> And now, &#91;though&#93; youi would needs be gone, because youi &#91;have&#93; sore longed after youri father’s house, &#91;yet&#93; For what reason hast youi stolen my gods?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> And Ya’akov answered and said to Lavan, Because I was afraid: for I said, Perhaps youi would take by force youri daughters from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> With whomsoever youi find youri gods, let him not live: before our brethren discern youi what &#91;is&#93; yoursi with me, and take &#91;it&#93; to youi. For Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) knew not that Rachel (ewe) had stolen them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And Lavan went into Ya’akov’s tent, and into Leah’s tent, and into the two maidservants’ tents; but he found &#91;them&#93; not. Then went he out of Leah’s tent, and entered into Rachel’s &#91;ewe&#93; (ewe) tent.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Now Rachel had taken the images, and put them in the camel’s furniture, and sat upon them. And Lavan (to be white; white guy) searched all the tent, but found &#91;them&#93; not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And she said to her father, Let it not displease my lord that I cannot rise up before youi; for the custom of women &#91;is&#93; upon me. And he searched, but found not the images.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) was exceedingly angry &#91;exceedingly angry&#93;, and expressed disapproval &#91;scolded, reproached&#93; with Lavan: and Ya’akov answered and said to Lavan, What &#91;is&#93; my trespass? what &#91;is&#93; my sin, that youi have so hotly pursued after me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Whereas youi have searched all my stuff, what have youi found of all youri household stuff? set &#91;it&#93; here before my brethren and youri brethren, that they may judge between us both.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> This twenty years &#91;have&#93; I &#91;been&#93; with youi; youri ewes and youri she goats have not cast their young, and the rams of youri flock have I not eaten.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> That which was torn &#91;of beasts&#93; I brought not unto youi; I bore the loss of it; of my hand did youi require it, &#91;whether&#93; stolen by day, or stolen by night.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> &#91;In the following manner&#93; I was; in the day the drought consumed me, and the frost by night; and my sleep departed from my eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> In the following manner have I been twenty years in youri house; I served youi fourteen years for youri two daughters, and six years for youri cattle: and youi hast changed my wages ten times.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> Except the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of my father, the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, and the fear of Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, had been with me, surely youi had sent me away now empty. God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has seen my affliction and the labour of my hands, and rebuked &#91;youi&#93; last night.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And Lavan answered and said unto Ya’akov, &#91;These&#93; daughters &#91;are&#93; my daughters, and &#91;these&#93; children &#91;are&#93; my children, and &#91;these&#93; cattle &#91;are&#93; my cattle, and all that youi see &#91;is&#93; my: and what can I do this day unto these my daughters, or unto their children which they have born?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> Now therefore come youi, let us make a covenant, I and youi; and let it be for a witness between me and youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) took a stone, and set it up &#91;for&#93; a pillar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> And Ya’akov said unto his brethren, Gather stones; and they took stones, and made an heap: and they did eat there upon the heap.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> And Lavan called it Y’gar-Sahaduta &#91;pile of witness&#93;: but Ya’akov called it Gal-’Ed &#91;pile of witness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And Lavan said, This heap &#91;is&#93; a witness between me and youi this day. Therefore was the name of it called Gal-’Ed;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> And HaMitzpah; for he said, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) watch between me and youi, when we are absent one from another.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> If youi shall afflict my daughters, or if youi shall take &#91;other&#93; wives beside my daughters, no man &#91;is&#93; with us; see, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; witness between me and youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> And Lavan (to be white; white guy) said to Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of), Behold this heap, and behold &#91;this&#93; pillar, which I have cast between me and youi;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> This heap &#91;be&#93; witness, and &#91;this&#93; pillar &#91;be&#93; witness, that I will not pass over this heap to youi, and that youi shall not pass over this heap and this pillar unto me, for harm.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> The God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, and the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of Nokhor (a snort, a snorting; charred, scorched), the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of their father, judge between us. And Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) swore by the fear of his father Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54"> Then Ya’akov offered sacrifice upon the mount, and called his brethren to eat bread: and they did eat bread, and tarried all night in the mount.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> And early in the morning Lavan rose up, and kissed his sons and his daughters, and Blessed (Favored by God; appy; prosperous) them: and Lavan (to be white; white guy) departed, and returned unto his place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And Ya’akov went on his way, and the angels of God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; met him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And when Ya’akov saw them, he said, This &#91;is&#93; God-Elōhīm’s &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; host: and he called the name of that place Machanayim &#91;Two Camps&#93; (two camps).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) sent messengers before him to ‘Esav (hairy) his brother unto the land of Se’ir (hairy, shaggy; The Hairy Guys; riding the storm), the country of Edom (red, ruddy (reddish hair or complexion)).</span>

<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16; Tehillim - Psalms 147:9"> Chieftain Dishon (thresher; fatness; ashes), chieftain Etzer (helper; treasure), chieftain Dishan (threshing; a thresher): these &#91;are&#93; the chieftains &#91;that came&#93; of Hori (cave dweller; freeborn prince), among their chieftains in the land of Se’ir (hairy, shaggy; The Hairy Guys; riding the storm).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> And these &#91;are&#93; the kings that reigned in the land of Edom (red, ruddy (reddish hair or complexion)), before there reigned any king over the children of Isra’el (he who holds onto God-The Creator).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And Bela (destruction) the son of B’or reigned in Edom (red, ruddy (reddish hair or complexion)): and the name of his city was Dinhabah (he gives judgment).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And Bela (destruction) died, and Yovav (To Call Shrilly) the son of Zerach (Rising; Dawning, Shining, Arising) of Botzrah (in tribulation or distress; fortress, sheepfold) reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And Yovav (To Call Shrilly) died, and Husham (haste) of the land of Temani (southward) reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And Husham (haste) died, and Hadad &#91;mighty&#93; (mighty; joy; noise; clamor) the son of B’dad (alone; solitary), who struck Midyan &#91;Strife&#93; (Strife; Place Of Judgment) in the field of Mo’av (of his father; Who’s Your Daddy!?), reigned in his stead: and the name of his city &#91;was&#93; ‘Avit (ruins).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And Hadad &#91;mighty&#93; (mighty; joy; noise; clamor) died, and Samlah (his clothing; his left hand; his astonishment) of Masrekah (vineyard of noble vines) reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And Samlah (his clothing; his left hand; his astonishment) died, and Sha’ul (desired; asked for) of Rekhovot (wide open spaces, wild places/streets) by-the-river reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And Sha’ul (desired; asked for) died, and Ba’al-Chanan (lord of grace; gracious husband) the son of ‘Akhbor (a rat; a mouse; bruising) reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And Ba’al-Chanan (lord of grace; gracious husband) the son of ‘Akhbor (a rat; a mouse; bruising) died, and Hadad &#91;mighty&#93; (honour) reigned in his stead: and the name of his city was Pau (bleating; howling; sighing); and his wife’s name was M’heitav’el (how good is God, favoured of God &#91;EL&#93;), the daughter of Matred (pushing forward; wand of government; expulsion), the daughter of Mei-Zahav (waters of gold; gilded).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And these &#91;are&#93; the names of the chieftains &#91;that came&#93; of ‘Esav (hairy), according to their families, after their places, by their names; chieftain Timnah &#91;Portion&#93; (restrained; forbidding), chieftain ‘Alvah (evil; his rising up; his highness), chieftain Y’tet (terror),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> Chieftain K’naz (hunter), chieftain Teman &#91;south&#93; (right hand; south), chieftain Mivtzar (fortress; defending; forbidding; taking away),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Chieftain Magdi’el (declaring God; chosen fruit of God; prince of God &#91;EL&#93;), chieftain ‘Iram (belonging to a city; a high heap; “fluid escaping into a body cavity” to them): these &#91;be&#93; the chieftains of Edom (red, ruddy (reddish hair or complexion)), according to their habitations in the land of their possession: he is ‘Esav (hairy) the father of the Edomim.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) dwelt in the land wherein his father was a stranger, in the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> These &#91;are&#93; the generations of Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of). Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;), &#91;being&#93; seventeen years old, was feeding the flock with his brethren; and the boy &#91;was&#93; with the sons of Bilhah (troubled; timid), and with the sons of Zilpah (a tickling; a sprinkle), his father’s wives: and Yosef brought unto his father their evil report.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> Now Isra’el loved Yosef more than all his children, because he &#91;was&#93; the son of his old age: and he made him a coat of &#91;many&#93; colors.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And when his brethren saw that their father loved him more than all his brethren, they hated him, and could not speak peaceably unto him.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) dreamed a dream, and he told &#91;it&#93; his brethren: and they hated him yet the more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And he said unto them, Hear, I pray you, this dream which I have dreamed:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> For, behold, we &#91;were&#93; binding sheaves in the field, and, lo, my sheaf arose, and also stood upright; and, behold, youri sheaves stood round about, and made obesiance (bowing or kneeling in respect) to my sheaf.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 9:1; Genesis 9:7; Leviticus 26:9"> Then there passed by Midyanim &#91;Strife&#93; (People of Strife; Place Of Judgment) merchantmen; and they drew and lifted up Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) out of the pit, and sold Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) to the Yishma’elim (They Will Hear God &#91;EL&#93;) for twenty &#91;pieces&#93; of silver: and they brought Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>

</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And Y’hudah (Let the Powerful One be Praised &#91;Yah&#93;) took a wife for ‘Er (watchful; watchman) his firstborn, whose name was Tamar &#91;palm-tree&#93; (bitterness, palm tree).</span>

<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And ‘Er (watchful; watchman), Y’hudah’s (Let the Powerful One be Praised &#91;Yah&#93;) firstborn, was wicked in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) killed him.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> And he returned to Y’hudah (Let the Powerful One be Praised &#91;Yah&#93;), and said, I cannot find her; and also the men of the place said, &#91;that&#93; there was no prostitute in this &#91;place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And Y’hudah said, Let her take &#91;it&#93; to her, lest we be shamed: behold, I sent this kid, and youi hast not found her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 50:9–10; Genesis 6:20; Genesis 8:19"> And it came to pass about three months after, that it was told Y’hudah, saying, Tamar &#91;palm-tree&#93; (bitterness, palm tree) youri daughter in law has played the prostitute; and also, behold, she &#91;is&#93; with child by whoredom. And Y’hudah (Let the Powerful One be Praised &#91;Yah&#93;) said, Bring her forth, and let her be burnt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Jeremiah 27:5; Job 12:8–10; Genesis 2:19–20"> When she &#91;was&#93; brought forth, she sent to her father in law, saying, By the man, whose these &#91;are, am&#93; I with child: and she said, Discern, I pray youi, whose &#91;are&#93; these, the signet (seal or stamp), and bracelets, and staff.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Colossians 3:10; Ephesians 4:24; Tehillim - Psalms 8:4–8"> And Y’hudah (Let the Powerful One be Praised &#91;Yah&#93;) acknowledged &#91;them&#93;, and said, She has been more righteous than I; because that I gave her not to Shelah (Sent Out, Branch or Javelin; to prosper, rest) my son. And he knew her again no more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 19:4; Ephesians 2:10; Genesis 5:1–2"> And it came to pass in the time of her travail, that, behold, twins &#91;were&#93; in her womb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 9:1; Genesis 9:7; Leviticus 26:9"> And it came to pass, when she travailed, that &#91;the one&#93; put out &#91;his&#93; hand: and the midwife took and bound upon his hand a scarlet thread, saying, This came out first.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Genesis 9:3; Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16"> And it came to pass, as he drew back his hand, that, behold, his brother came out: and she said, How hast youi broken forth? &#91;this&#93; breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet) &#91;be&#93; upon youi: therefore his name was called Peretz (a breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet); divided; rupture).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16; Tehillim - Psalms 147:9"> And afterward came out his brother, that had the scarlet thread upon his hand: and his name was called Zerach (a rising &#91;as the sun&#93;, scarlet).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) was brought down to Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land); and Potifar (He Whom The Ra Gave, Priest of the Bull, belonging to the sun), an officer of Pharaoh (Great House), captain of the guard, an Mitzrayimot (People of the Black Land), bought him of the hands of the Yishma’elim (They Will Hear God &#91;EL&#93;), which had brought him down there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was with Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;), and he was a prosperous man; and he was in the house of his master the Mitzrayimot.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And his master saw that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;was&#93; with him, and that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) made all that he did to prosper in his hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And Yosef found grace in his sight, and he served him: and he made him overseer over his house, and all &#91;that&#93; he had he put into his hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> And it came to pass from the time &#91;that&#93; he had made him overseer in his house, and over all that he had, that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the Mitzrayimi house for Yosef’s sake; and the blessing of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was upon all that he had in the house, and in the field.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And he left all that he had in Yosef’s (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) hand; and he knew not ought he had, save the bread which he did eat. And Yosef was &#91;a&#93; goodly &#91;person&#93;, and well favoured.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And it came to pass after these things, that his master’s wife cast her eyes upon Yosef; and she said, Lie with me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> But he refused, and said unto his master’s wife, Behold, my master knows not what &#91;is&#93; with me in the house, and he has committed all that he has to my hand;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> &#91;There is&#93; none greater in this house than I; neither has he kept back anything from me but youi, because youi &#91;are&#93; his wife: how then can I do this great wickedness, and sin against God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> And it came to pass, as she spoke to Yosef day by day, that he listened not unto her, to lie by her, &#91;or&#93; to be with her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And it came to pass about this time, that &#91;Yosef&#93; went into the house to do his business; and &#91;there was&#93; none of the men of the house there within.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And she caught him by his garment, saying, Lie with me: and he left his garment in her hand, and fled, and got him out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> And it came to pass, when she saw that he had left his garment in her hand, and was fled forth,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> That she called unto the men of her house, and spoke unto them, saying, See, he has brought in an Hebrew unto us to mock us; he came in unto me to lie with me, and I cried with a loud voice:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And it came to pass, when he heard that I lifted up my voice and cried, that he left his garment with me, and fled, and got him out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> And she laid up his garment by her, until his lord came home.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> And she spoke unto him according to these words, saying, The Hebrew servant, which youi have brought unto us, came in unto me to mock me:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> And it came to pass, as I lifted up my voice and cried, that he left his garment with me, and fled out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And it came to pass, when his master heard the words of his wife, which she spoke unto him, saying, After this manner did youri servant to me; that his wrath was kindled.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 2:19; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–25; 1 Kings 4:33"> And Yosef’s (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) master took him, and put him into the prison, a place where the king’s prisoners &#91;were&#93; bound: and he was there in the prison.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–26; Genesis 1:25; Genesis 8:19"> But the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was with Yosef, and showed him mercy, and gave him favour in the sight of the keeper of the prison.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> And the keeper of the prison committed to Yosef’s hand all the prisoners that &#91;were&#93; in the prison; and whatsoever they did there, he was the doer &#91;of it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> The keeper of the prison looked not to anything &#91;that was&#93; under his hand; because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was with him, and &#91;that&#93; which he did, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) made &#91;it&#93; to prosper.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And it came to pass after these things, &#91;that&#93; the butler of the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land) and &#91;his&#93; baker had offended their lord the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And Pharaoh (Great House) was exceedingly angry against two &#91;of&#93; his officers, against the chief of the butlers, and against the chief of the bakers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And he put them in ward (in custody, with a guard; prison) in the house of the captain of the guard, into the prison, the place where Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) &#91;was&#93; bound.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And the captain of the guard charged Yosef with them, and he served them: and they continued a season in ward (in custody, with a guard; prison).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> And they dreamed a dream both of them, each man his dream in one night, each man according to the interpretation of his dream, the butler and the baker of the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;, which &#91;were&#93; bound in the prison.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And Yosef came in unto them in the morning, and looked upon them, and, behold, they &#91;were&#93; sad.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And he asked Pharaoh’s officers that &#91;were&#93; with him in the ward (in custody, with a guard; prison) of his lord’s house, saying, For what reason look youf &#91;so&#93; sadly to day?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And they said unto him, We have dreamed a dream, and &#91;there is&#93; no interpreter of it. And Yosef said unto them, &#91;Do&#93; not interpretations &#91;belong&#93; to God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;? tell me &#91;them&#93;, I pray you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> And the chief butler told his dream to Yosef, and said to him, In my dream, behold, a vine &#91;was&#93; before me;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> And in the vine &#91;were&#93; three branches: and it &#91;was&#93; as though it budded, &#91;and&#93; her blossoms shot forth; and the clusters thereof brought forth ripe grapes:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And Pharaoh’s cup &#91;was&#93; in my hand: and I took the grapes, and pressed them into Pharaoh’s cup, and I gave the cup into Pharaoh’s hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And Yosef said unto him, This &#91;is&#93; the interpretation of it: The three branches &#91;are&#93; three days:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Yet within three days shall Pharaoh lift up youri head, and restore youi unto youri place: and youi shall deliver Pharaoh’s cup into his hand, after the former manner when youi were his butler.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> But think on me when it shall be well with youi, and show kindness, I pray youi, unto me, and make mention of me unto Pharaoh (Great House), and bring me out of this house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> For indeed I was stolen away out of the land of the Hebrews: and here also have I done nothing that they should put me into the dungeon.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> When the chief baker saw that the interpretation was good, he said unto Yosef, I also &#91;was&#93; in my dream, and, behold, &#91;I had&#93; three white baskets on my head:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> And in the uppermost basket &#91;there was&#93; of all manner of bakemeats &#91;works of the baker, such as biscuits and cakes&#93; for Pharaoh; and the birds did eat them out of the basket upon my head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> And Yosef answered and said, This &#91;is&#93; the interpretation thereof: The three baskets &#91;are&#93; three days:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Yet within three days shall Pharaoh lift up youri head from off youi, and shall hang youi on a tree; and the birds shall eat youri flesh from off youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 2:19; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–25; 1 Kings 4:33"> And it came to pass the third day, &#91;which was&#93; Pharaoh’s birthday, that he made a feast unto all his servants: and he lifted up the head of the chief butler and of the chief baker among his servants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–26; Genesis 1:25; Genesis 8:19"> And he restored the chief butler unto his butlership again; and he gave the cup into Pharaoh’s hand:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> But he hanged the chief baker: as Yosef had interpreted to them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Yet did not the chief butler remember Yosef, but forgot him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And it came to pass at the end of two full years, that Pharaoh (Great House) dreamed: and, behold, he stood by the river.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And, behold, there came up out of the river seven well favoured cows and fatfleshed &#91;plump&#93;; and they fed in a meadow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And, behold, seven other cows came up after them out of the river, ill favoured and leanfleshed &#91;emaciated&#93;; and stood by the &#91;other&#93; cows upon the brink of the river.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And the ill favoured and leanfleshed cows did eat up the seven well favoured and fat cows. So Pharaoh awoke.</span>
</div>
