<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> And he slept and dreamed the second time: and, behold, seven ears of corn came up upon one stalk, rank and good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And, behold, seven thin ears and blasted with the east wind sprung up after them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And the seven thin ears devoured the seven rank and full ears. And Pharaoh awoke, and, behold, &#91;it was&#93; a dream.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And it came to pass in the morning that his spirit was troubled; and he sent and called for all the magicians of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;, and all the wise men thereof: and Pharaoh told them his dream; but &#91;there was&#93; none that could interpret them unto Pharaoh.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> Then spoke the chief butler unto Pharaoh, saying, I do remember my faults this day:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> Pharaoh was exceedingly angry with his servants, and put me in ward (in custody, with a guard; prison) in the captain of the guard’s house, &#91;both&#93; me and the chief baker:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And we dreamed a dream in one night, I and he; we dreamed each man according to the interpretation of his dream.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And &#91;there was&#93; there with us a young man, an Hebrew, servant to the captain of the guard; and we told him, and he interpreted to us our dreams; to each man according to his dream he did interpret.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> And it came to pass, as he interpreted to us, so it was; me he restored unto my office, and him he hanged.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> Then Pharaoh (Great House) sent and called Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;), and they brought him hastily out of the dungeon: and he shaved &#91;himself&#93;, and changed his clothing, and came in unto Pharaoh.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And Pharaoh said unto Yosef, I have dreamed a dream, and &#91;there is&#93; none that can interpret it: and I have heard say of youi, &#91;that&#93; youi can understand a dream to interpret it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> And Yosef answered Pharaoh, saying, &#91;It is&#93; not in me: God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; shall give Pharaoh an answer of peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> And Pharaoh said unto Yosef, In my dream, behold, I stood upon the bank of the river:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> And, behold, there came up out of the river seven cows, fatfleshed and well favoured; and they fed in a meadow:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And, behold, seven other cows came up after them, poor and very ill favoured and leanfleshed, such as I never saw in all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; for badness:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 2:19; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–25; 1 Kings 4:33"> And the lean and the ill favoured cows did eat up the first seven fat cows:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–26; Genesis 1:25; Genesis 8:19"> And when they had eaten them up, it could not be known that they had eaten them; but they &#91;were&#93; still ill favoured, as at the beginning. So I awoke.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> And I saw in my dream, and, behold, seven ears came up in one stalk, full and good:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And, behold, seven ears, withered, thin, &#91;and&#93; blasted with the east wind, sprung up after them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 50:9–10; Genesis 6:20; Genesis 8:19"> And the thin ears devoured the seven good ears: and I told &#91;this&#93; unto the magicians; but &#91;there was&#93; none that could declare &#91;it&#93; to me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Jeremiah 27:5; Job 12:8–10; Genesis 2:19–20"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) said unto Pharaoh (Great House), The dream of Pharaoh &#91;is&#93; one: God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has showed Pharaoh what he &#91;is&#93; about to do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Colossians 3:10; Ephesians 4:24; Tehillim - Psalms 8:4–8"> The seven good cows &#91;are&#93; seven years; and the seven good ears &#91;are&#93; seven years: the dream &#91;is&#93; one.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 19:4; Ephesians 2:10; Genesis 5:1–2"> And the seven thin and ill favoured cows that came up after them &#91;are&#93; seven years; and the seven empty ears blasted with the east wind shall be seven years of famine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 9:1; Genesis 9:7; Leviticus 26:9"> This &#91;is&#93; the thing which I have spoken unto Pharaoh: What God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; about to do he shows unto Pharaoh.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Genesis 9:3; Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16"> Behold, there come seven years of great plenty throughout all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16; Tehillim - Psalms 147:9"> And there shall arise after them seven years of famine; and all the plenty shall be forgotten in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land); and the famine shall consume the land;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> And the plenty shall not be known in the land by reason of that famine following; for it &#91;shall be&#93; very grievous.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And for that the dream was doubled unto Pharaoh twice; &#91;it is&#93; because the thing &#91;is&#93; established by God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;, and God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; will shortly bring it to pass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Now therefore let Pharaoh (Great House) look out a man discreet and wise, and set him over the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Let Pharaoh do &#91;this&#93;, and let him appoint officers over the land, and take up the fifth part of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; in the seven plenteous years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And let them gather all the food of those good years that come, and lay up corn under the hand of Pharaoh, and let them keep food in the cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And that food shall be for store to the land against the seven years of famine, which shall be in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;; that the land perish not through the famine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And the thing was good in the eyes of Pharaoh (Great House), and in the eyes of all his servants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And Pharaoh (Great House) said unto his servants, Can we find &#91;such a one&#93; as this &#91;is&#93;, a man in whom the Spirit of God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And Pharaoh (Great House) said unto Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;), Forasmuch as God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has showed youi all this, &#91;there is&#93; none so discreet and wise as youi &#91;are&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> Youi shall be over my house, and according unto youri word shall all my people be ruled: only in the throne will I be greater than youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And Pharaoh (Great House) said unto Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;), See, I have set youi over all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> And Pharaoh (Great House) took off his ring from his hand, and put it upon Yosef’s (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) hand, and arrayed him in vestures of fine linen, and put a gold chain about his neck;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And he made him to ride in the second chariot which he had; and they cried before him, Bow the knee: and he made him &#91;ruler&#93; over all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And Pharaoh (Great House) said unto Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;), I am Pharaoh (Great House), and without youi shall no man lift up his hand or foot in all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And Pharaoh called Yosef’s (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) name Tzafnat-Pa’neach (Treasury of the Glorious Rest; Revealer of the Secrets of Life): and he gave him to wife Asenath (gift of the water) the daughter of Osnat the daughter of Poti-Fera priest of On (belonging to the sun-god; he whom Ra gave) &#91;On = meaning sun-city; Heliopolis&#93;. And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) went out over &#91;all&#93; the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) &#91;was&#93; thirty years old when he stood before Pharaoh (Great House) king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land). And Yosef went out from the presence of Pharaoh, and went throughout all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> And in the seven plenteous years the earth brought forth by handfuls.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And he gathered up all the food of the seven years, which were in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;, and laid up the food in the cities: the food of the field, which &#91;was&#93; round about every city, laid he up in the same.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> And Yosef gathered corn as the sand of the sea, very much, until he left numbering; for &#91;it was&#93; without number.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> And unto Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) were born two sons before the years of famine came, which Osnat (gift of the water) the daughter of Poti-Fera (belonging to the sun-god; he whom Ra gave) priest of On (meaning sun-city; Heliopolis) bore unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) called the name of the firstborn M’nasheh (causing to forget; from a debt): For God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;said he&#93;, has made me forget all my toil, and all my father’s house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> And the name of the second he called Efrayim (Out of the Ashes I am Fruitful; fruitful in affliction): For God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has caused me to be fruitful in the land of my affliction.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> And the seven years of plenteousness, that was in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land), were ended.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54"> And the seven years of famine began to ome, according as Yosef had said: and the famine was in all lands; but in all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; there was bread.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> And when all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land) was famished, the people cried to Pharaoh (Great House) for bread: and Pharaoh said unto all the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93; (People of the Black Land), Go unto Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;); what he says to you, do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> And the famine was over all the face of the earth: And Yosef opened all the storehouses, and sold unto the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93;Egyptians; and the famine waxed sore in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> And all countries came into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; to Yosef for to buy &#91;corn&#93;; because that the famine was &#91;so&#93; sore in all lands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> Now when Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) saw that there was corn in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land), Ya’akov said unto his sons, Why do youf look one upon another?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And he said, Behold, I have heard that there is corn in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;: get you down there, and buy for us from there; that we may live, and not die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> Yosef’s (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) ten brethren went down to buy corn in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> But Binyamin (This Son Is Strength), Yosef’s (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) brother, Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) sent not with his brethren; for he said, Lest perhaps mischief befall him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> And the sons of Isra’el (he who holds onto God-The Creator) came to buy &#91;corn&#93; among those that came: for the famine was in the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And Yosef &#91;was&#93; the governor over the land, &#91;and&#93; he &#91;it was&#93; that sold to all the people of the land: and Yosef’s brethren came, and bowed down themselves before him &#91;with&#93; their faces to the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And Yosef saw his brethren, and he knew them, but made himself strange unto them, and spoke roughly unto them; and he said unto them, Whence come youf? And they said, From the land of Canaan to buy food.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And Yosef knew his brethren, but they knew not him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> And Yosef remembered the dreams which he dreamed of them, and said unto them, You1sf &#91;are&#93; spies; to see the nakedness of the land you1sf are come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> And they said unto him, No, my lord, but to buy food are youri servants come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> We &#91;are&#93; all one man’s sons; we &#91;are&#93; true &#91;men&#93;, youri servants are no spies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And he said unto them, No, but to see the nakedness of the land youf are come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> And they said, youri servants &#91;are&#93; twelve brethren, the sons of one man in the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues); and, behold, the youngest &#91;is&#93; this day with our father, and one &#91;is&#93; not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) said unto them, That &#91;is it&#93; that I spoke unto you, saying, You1sf &#91;are&#93; spies:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Hereby you1sf shall be proved: By the life of Pharaoh (Great House) you1sf shall not go forth hence, except your youngest brother come to this place (till this point in time).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> Send one of you, and let him fetch your brother, and you1sf shall be kept in prison, that your words may be proved, whether &#91;there be any&#93; tin you: or else by the life of Pharaoh surely you1sf &#91;are&#93; spies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> And he put them all to (to do entirely, do wholly) gether into ward (in custody, with a guard; prison) three days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> And Yosef said unto them the third day, This do, and live; &#91;for&#93; I fear God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> If you1sf &#91;be&#93; true &#91;men&#93;, let one of your brethren be bound in the house of your prison: go you1sf, carry corn for the famine of your houses:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 2:19; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–25; 1 Kings 4:33"> But bring your youngest brother unto me; so shall your words be verified, and youf shall not die. And they did so.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–26; Genesis 1:25; Genesis 8:19"> And they said one to another, We &#91;are&#93; truly guilty concerning our brother, in that we saw the anguish of his soul, when he besought us, and we would not hear; therefore is this distress come upon us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> And Re’uven (behold a son) answered them, saying, Spoke I not unto you, saying, Do not sin against the child; and youf would not hear? therefore, behold, also his blood is required.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And they knew not that Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) understood &#91;them&#93;; for he spoke unto them by an interpreter.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 50:9–10; Genesis 6:20; Genesis 8:19"> And he turned himself about from them, and wept; and returned to them again, and communed with them, and took from them Shim’on (heard; he who hears; man of hearing), and bound him before their eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Jeremiah 27:5; Job 12:8–10; Genesis 2:19–20"> Then Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) commanded to fill their sacks with corn, and to restore every man’s money into his sack, and to give them provision for the way: and in this way did he unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Colossians 3:10; Ephesians 4:24; Tehillim - Psalms 8:4–8"> And they laded their donkeys with the corn, and departed there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 19:4; Ephesians 2:10; Genesis 5:1–2"> And as one of them opened his sack to give his donkey provender (food for livestock or cattle) in the inn, he discovered (spied out; examined) his money; for, behold, it &#91;was&#93; in his sack’s mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 9:1; Genesis 9:7; Leviticus 26:9"> And he said unto his brethren, My money is restored; and, lo, &#91;it is&#93; even in my sack: and their heart failed &#91;them&#93;, and they were afraid, saying one to another, What &#91;is&#93; this &#91;that&#93; God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has done unto us?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Genesis 9:3; Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16"> And they came unto Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) their father unto the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues), and told him all that befell unto them; saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16; Tehillim - Psalms 147:9"> The man, &#91;who is&#93; the lord of the land, spoke roughly to us, and took us for spies of the country.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> And we said unto him, We &#91;are&#93; true &#91;men&#93;; we are no spies:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> We &#91;be&#93; twelve brethren, sons of our father; one &#91;is&#93; not, and the youngest &#91;is&#93; this day with our father in the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And the man, the lord of the country, said unto us, Hereby shall I know that you1sf &#91;are&#93; true &#91;men&#93;; leave one of yourf brethren &#91;here&#93; with me, and take &#91;food for&#93; the famine of yourf households, and be gone:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And bring your youngest brother unto me: then shall I know that you1sf &#91;are&#93; not spies, but &#91;that&#93; you1sf &#91;are&#93; true &#91;men: so&#93; will I deliver &#91;to&#93; youf yourf brother, and you1sf shall traffick (trade; do business) &#91;engage in illegal activity&#93; in the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And it came to pass as they emptied their sacks, that, behold, every man’s bundle of money &#91;was&#93; in his sack: and when &#91;both&#93; they and their father saw the bundles of money, they were afraid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) their father said unto them, Me have youf bereaved &#91;of my children&#93;: Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) &#91;is&#93; not, and Shim’on (heard; he who hears; man of hearing) &#91;is&#93; not, and you1sf will take Binyamin (This Son Is Strength) &#91;away&#93;: all these things are against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And Re’uven (behold a son) spoke unto his father, saying, Slay my two sons, if I bring him not to youi: deliver him into my hand, and I will bring him to youi again.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And he said, My son shall not go down with you; for his brother is dead, and he is left alone: if mischief befall him by the way in the which you1sf go, then shall you1sf bring down my gray hairs with sorrow to the grave.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And the famine &#91;was&#93; sore in the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And it came to pass, when they had eaten up the corn which they had brought out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land), their father said unto them, Go again, buy us a little food.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And Y’hudah (Let the Powerful One be Praised &#91;Yah&#93;) spoke unto him, saying, The man did solemnly protest unto us, saying, You1sf shall not see my face, except your brother &#91;be&#93; with you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> If youi will send our brother with us, we will go down and buy youi food:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> But if youi will not send &#91;him&#93;, we will not go down: for the man said unto us, You1sf shall not see my face, except your brother &#91;be&#93; with you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And Isra’el (he who holds onto God-The Creator) said, For what reason have you1sf dealt &#91;so&#93; ill with me, &#91;as&#93; to tell the man whether you1sf had yet a brother?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And they said, The man asked us rigorously of our state, and of our family, saying, &#91;Is&#93; yourf father yet alive? have youf &#91;another&#93; brother? and we told him according to the tenor of these words: could we certainly know that he would say, Bring your brother down?</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And Y’hudah (Let the Powerful One be Praised &#91;Yah&#93;) said unto Isra’el (he who holds onto God-The Creator) his father, Send the boy with me, and we will arise and go; that we may live, and not die, both we, and youi, &#91;and&#93; also our little ones.</span>

If I be bereaved &#91;of my children&#93;, I am bereaved.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And the men took that present, and they took double money in their hand, and Binyamin; and rose up, and went down to Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;, and stood before Yosef.</span>

<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> And when Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) saw Binyamin (This Son Is Strength) with them, he said to the ruler of his house, Bring &#91;these&#93; men home, and slay, and make ready; for &#91;these&#93; men shall dine with me at noon.</span>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And they set on for him by himself, and for them by themselves, and for the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93;, which did eat with him, by themselves: because the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93; (People of the Black Land) might not eat bread with the Hebrews; for that &#91;is&#93; an abomination unto the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And they sat before him, the firstborn according to his birthright, and the youngest according to his youth: and the men marveled one at another.</span>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> Then Y’hudah came near unto him, and said, Oh my lord, let youi servant, I pray youi, speak a word in my lord’s ears, and let not youri anger burn against youri servant: for youi &#91;are&#93; even as Pharaoh (Great House).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> My lord asked his servants, saying, Have youf a father, or a brother?</span>

<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–26; Genesis 1:25; Genesis 8:19"> And youi said unto youi servants, Bring him down unto me, that I may set my eyes upon him.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Colossians 3:10; Ephesians 4:24; Tehillim - Psalms 8:4–8"> And we said, We cannot go down: if our youngest brother be with us, then will we go down: for we may not see the man’s face, except our youngest brother &#91;be&#93; with us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Gosel 19:4; Ephesians 2:10; Genesis 5:1–2"> And youri servant my father said unto us, You1sf know that my wife bore me two &#91;sons&#93;:</span>

<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 9:1; Genesis 9:7; Leviticus 26:9"> And the one went out from me, and I said, Surely he is torn in pieces; and I saw him not since:</span>

<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Genesis 9:3; Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–15; Tehillim - Psalms 145:15–16"> If you1sf take this also from me, and mischief befall him, you1sf shall bring down my gray hairs with sorrow to the grave.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="1 Timothy 4:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24; Tehillim - Psalms 19:1–2"> It shall come to pass, when he sees that the boy &#91;is&#93; not &#91;with us&#93;, that he will die: and youri servants shall bring down the gray hairs of youri servant our father with sorrow to the grave.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> For youri servant became surety for the boy unto my father, saying, If I bring him not unto youi, then I shall bear the blame to my father forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Now therefore, I pray youi, let youri servant abide instead of the boy a bondman (slave) to my lord; and let the boy go up with his brethren.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; sent me before youi to preserve youi a posterity in the earth, and to save youri lives by a great deliverance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> So now &#91;it was&#93; not you &#91;that&#93; sent me to this place (till this point in time), but God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;: and he has made me a father to Pharaoh (Great House), and lord of all his house, and a ruler throughout all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> Haste youf, and go up to my father, and say unto him, In the following manner says youri son Yosef, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; has made me lord of all Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;: come down unto me, wait not:</span>

<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And there will I nourish youi; for yet &#91;there are&#93; five years of famine; lest youi, and youri household, and all that youi have, come to poverty.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> And you1sf shall tell my father of all my glory in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; and of all that you1sf have seen; and you1sf shall haste and bring down my father to this place (till this point in time).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) brought in Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) his father, and set him before Pharaoh (Great House): and Ya’akov Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) Pharaoh.</span>

<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> And Ya’akov said unto Pharaoh, The days of the years of my pilgrimage &#91;are&#93; an hundred and thirty years: few and evil have the days of the years of my life been, and have not attained unto the days of the years of the life of my fathers in the days of their pilgrimage.</span>

<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And Yosef placed his father and his brethren, and gave them a possession in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; in the best of the land, in the land of Ra’amses &#91;child or born of the sun god Ra&#93;, as Pharaoh (Great House) had commanded.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> And &#91;there was&#93; no bread in all the land; for the famine &#91;was&#93; very sore, so that the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; &#91;all&#93; the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues) fainted by reason of the famine.</span>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> And Yosef gathered up all the money that was found in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;, and in the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues), for the corn which they bought: and Yosef brought the money into Pharaoh’s house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And when money failed in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;, and in the land of Kena’an all the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93; (People of the Black Land) came unto Yosef, and said, Give us bread: for why should we die in youri presence? for the money fails.</span>

<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> And they brought their cattle unto Yosef: and Yosef gave them bread &#91;in exchange&#93; for horses, and for the flocks, and for the cattle of the herds, and for the donkeys: and he fed them with bread for all their cattle for that year.</span>

<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And when Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of) had made an end of commanding his sons, he gathered up his feet into the bed, and yielded up the ghost, and was gathered unto his people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="John 1:1–3; Hebrews 11:3; Isaiah 45:18"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) fell upon his father’s face, and wept upon him, and kissed him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Jeremiah 4:23; Tehillim - Psalms 104:30; Isaiah 45:18"> And Yosef commanded his servants the physicians to embalm his father: and the physicians embalmed Isra’el (he who holds onto God-The Creator).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="2 Corinthians 4:6; John 1:5; Isaiah 45:7"> And forty days were fulfilled for him; for so are fulfilled the days of those which are embalmed: and the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93; (People of the Black Land) mourned for him threescore and ten days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 2:13; Genesis 1:18; Ecclesiastes 11:7"> And when the days of his mourning were past, Yosef spoke unto the house of Pharaoh, saying, If now I have found grace in youri eyes, speak, I pray you, in the ears of Pharaoh, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 74:16; Isaiah 45:7; 1 Thessalonians 5:5"> My father made me swear, saying, Lo, I die: in my grave which I have dug for me in the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues), there shall youi bury me. Now therefore let me go up, I pray youi, and bury my father, and I will come again.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 51:15; Tehillim - Psalms 33:6; Job 37:18"> And Pharaoh (Great House) said, Go up, and bury youri father, according as he made youi swear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 8:28–29; Tehillim - Psalms 148:4; Gosel 8:27"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) went up to bury his father: and with him went up all the servants of Pharaoh, the elders of his house, and all the elders of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 1:5; Genesis 1:19; Genesis 1:10"> And all the house of Yosef, and his brethren, and his father’s house: only their little ones, and their flocks, and their herds, they left in the land of Goshen &#91;Drawing Near&#93; (approaching; drawing near).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Peter 3:5; Jonah 1:9; Jeremiah 5:22"> And there went up with him both chariots and horsemen: and it was a very great company.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:4; Tehillim - Psalms 104:31; Genesis 1:4"> And they came to the threshing floor of Atad (a thorn), which &#91;is&#93; beyond Yarden (The Descender, Descending), and there they mourned with a great and very sore lamentation: and he made a mourning for his father seven days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:14–17; Hebrews 6:7; Tehillim - Psalms 147:8"> And when the inhabitants of the land, the Kena’ani (low/humbled ones; merchants), saw the mourning in the floor of Atad (a thorn), they said, This &#91;is&#93; a grievous mourning to the Mitzrayimot &#91;Egyptians&#93; (People of the Black Land): For what reason the name of it was called Avel-Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (meadow of the Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land &#91;Mitzrayim&#93; &#91;Egypt&#93; which &#91;is&#93; beyond Yarden &#91;The Descender, Descending&#93;).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mark 4:28; Isaiah 55:10–11; Gosel 13:24–26"> And his sons did unto him according as he commanded them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> For his sons carried him into the land of Kena’an (merchant; trader; or that humbles and subdues), and buried him in the cave of the field of Makhpelah (portion; double cave), which Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93; bought with the field for a possession of a burying place of ‘Efron (fawn-like) the Hitti (terrorists), before Mamre (firmness; fatling (fat cattle) offering).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:19–20; Deuteronomy 4:19; Jeremiah 31:35"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) returned into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land), he, and his brethren, and all that went up with him to bury his father, after he had buried his father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And when Yosef’s brethren saw that their father was dead, they said, Yosef will perhaps hate us, and will certainly requite (repay) us all the evil which we did unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 136:7–9; Deuteronomy 4:19; Tehillim - Psalms 8:3"> And they sent a messenger unto Yosef, saying, youri father did command before he died, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 8:3; Tehillim - Psalms 8:1; Job 38:12"> So shall youf say unto Yosef, Forgive, I pray youi now, the trespass of youri brethren, and their sin; for they did unto youi evil: and now, we pray youi, forgive the trespass of the servants of the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; of youri father. And Yosef wept when they spoke unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Jeremiah 31:35; Tehillim - Psalms 19:6"> And his brethren also went and fell down before his face; and they said, Behold, we &#91;be&#93; youri servants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And Yosef said unto them, Fear not: for &#91;am&#93; I in the place of God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 2:19; Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–25; 1 Kings 4:33"> But as for you, youf thought evil against me; &#91;but&#93; God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; meant it unto good, to bring to pass, as &#91;it is&#93; this day, to save much people alive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 104:24–26; Genesis 1:25; Genesis 8:19"> Now therefore fear youf not: I will nourish youi, and youri little ones. And he comforted them, and spoke kindly unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 40:15; Genesis 8:17; Tehillim - Psalms 107:38"> And Yosef dwelt in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93;, he, and his father’s house: and Yosef lived an hundred and ten years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And Yosef saw Efrayim’s children of the third &#91;generation&#93;: the children also of Makhir (sold; selling) the son of M’nasheh (causing to forget; from a debt) were brought up upon Yosef’s (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) knees.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 50:9–10; Genesis 6:20; Genesis 8:19"> And Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) said unto his brethren, I die: and God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; will surely visit you, and bring youi out of this land unto the land which he swore to Avraham &#91;exalted father/Fly They Will&#93;, to Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and to Ya’akov (he who holds onto the heel of).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Jeremiah 27:5; Job 12:8–10; Genesis 2:19–20"> And Yosef took an oath of the children of Isra’el (he who holds onto God-The Creator), saying, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word - The Many Powered&#93; will surely visit you, and youf shall carry up my bones from hence.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Colossians 3:10; Ephesians 4:24; Tehillim - Psalms 8:4–8"> So Yosef (Increaser; May God the Powerful One add &#91;Yah&#93;) died, &#91;being&#93; an hundred and ten years old: and they embalmed him, and he was put in a coffin in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt&#93; (Double Distress or Double Stronghold, Black Land).</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
